00001         * PACMAIN : DRIVER ONLY. NO GAME RULES, NO I/O STATEMENTS.
00002         * PACPLAT (HOST)  PACINPT (CARDS)  PACGAME (RULES)  PACMAP (MAP)
00003         * PACAI (GHOSTS)  PACDISP (TEXT OUTPUT). SEE DOCS/ARCHITECTURE.MD
00004         * PARM 'TSO' [AIL N] REQUESTS THE INTERACTIVE TSO ADAPTER
00005         * (MVS-8): CONFIG COMES FROM THE PARM, THE BOARD IS SHOWN BEFORE
00006         * THE FIRST PROMPT. NO PARM = BATCH CARD MODE (REFERENCE).
00007         * NO INPUT IS READ ONCE THE GAME HAS ENDED (QUIT, WON, LOST).
00008          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00009          PROGRAM-ID. PACMAIN.
00010          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00011          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00012          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00013          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00014          DATA DIVISION.
00015          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00016          01  PAC-STATE COPY PACSTATE.
00017 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00018 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00019 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00020 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00021 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00022 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.
00023 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00024 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00025 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00026 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00027 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00028 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00029 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00030 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00031 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00032 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00033 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00034 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00035 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00036 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00037 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00038 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00039 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00040 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00041 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00042 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00043 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00044          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00045 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00046 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00047 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00048 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00049 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00050 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00051 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00052 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00053 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00054 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).   2


00055          01  WS-CFG.
00056              05  WS-CFG-CH         PIC X OCCURS 5 TIMES.
00057          01  WS-K                  PIC 99.
00058          LINKAGE SECTION.
00059          01  PARM-AREA.
00060              05  PARM-LEN          PIC S9(4) COMP.
00061              05  PARM-MODE         PIC X(3).
00062              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00063              05  PARM-CFG          PIC X(5).
00064          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PARM-AREA.
00065          MAIN-PARA.
00066              MOVE 'BAT' TO REQ-MODE.
00067              IF PARM-LEN > 2 AND PARM-MODE = 'TSO'
00068                  MOVE 'TSO' TO REQ-MODE.
00069              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00070              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00071              MOVE 'RSET' TO REQ-OP.
00072              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00073              MOVE 'OPEN' TO REQ-OP.
00074              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00075              MOVE 'HEAD' TO REQ-OP.
00076              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00077              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00078                  PERFORM TSO-START THRU TSO-START-EXIT
00079              ELSE
00080                  PERFORM BAT-START THRU BAT-START-EXIT.
00081              PERFORM TURN-PARA THRU TURN-PARA-EXIT
00082                  UNTIL F-EOF = 1 OR F-QUIT = 1 OR F-WON = 1
00083                     OR F-LOST = 1.
00084              MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP.
00085              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00086              MOVE 'CLOS' TO REQ-OP.
00087              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00088              MOVE 'FINI' TO REQ-OP.
00089              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00090              STOP RUN.
00091          BAT-START.
00092              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00093              PERFORM CHECK-CONFIG THRU CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00094              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00095          BAT-START-EXIT.
00096              EXIT.
00097          TSO-START.
00098              IF PARM-LEN > 4
00099                  PERFORM COPY-PARM-CFG
00100                  MOVE 'PARM' TO REQ-OP
00101                  CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ
00102                  PERFORM APPLY-CONFIG THRU APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00103              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00104              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00105          TSO-START-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.
00107          COPY-PARM-CFG.
00108         * ONLY PARM-LEN BYTES ARE VALID: BLANK WHAT LIES BEYOND THEM.
00109              MOVE PARM-CFG TO WS-CFG.
00110              COMPUTE WS-K = PARM-LEN - 3.
00111              PERFORM BLANK-CFG-CH UNTIL WS-K > 5.   3


00112              MOVE WS-CFG TO REQ-TEXT.
00113          BLANK-CFG-CH.
00114              MOVE SPACE TO WS-CFG-CH (WS-K).
00115              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00116          SHOW-START.
00117              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00118              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00119              MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP.
00120              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00121          APPLY-CONFIG.
00122              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00123                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00124                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00125          APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00126              EXIT.
00127          READ-INPUT.
00128              MOVE 'PRMT' TO REQ-OP.
00129              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00130              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00131              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00132              IF REQ-KIND = 'EOF'
00133                  MOVE 'EOF ' TO REQ-OP
00134                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00135          READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00136              EXIT.
00137          CHECK-CONFIG.
00138              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00139                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00140                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00141                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00142          CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00143              EXIT.
00144          TURN-PARA.
00145              PERFORM DISPATCH THRU DISPATCH-EXIT.
00146              IF F-QUIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND F-LOST = 0
00147                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00148          TURN-PARA-EXIT.
00149              EXIT.
00150          DISPATCH.
00151              IF REQ-KIND = 'MOV'
00152                  MOVE 'MOVE' TO REQ-OP
00153                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00154                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00155                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00156              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'QUI'
00157                  MOVE 'QUIT' TO REQ-OP
00158                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00159              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'RDR'
00160                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00161                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00162              ELSE
00163                  MOVE 'INVL' TO REQ-OP
00164                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00165          DISPATCH-EXIT.
00166              EXIT.   4

00001         * PACGAME : GAME RULES AND STATE (PORTABLE, NO TERMINAL I/O).
00002         * OPS : RSET  DEFAULTS (3 LIVES, PLAYER 2,2, AI1, 1 GHOST)
00003         *       CFG   APPLY REQ-LEVEL / REQ-COUNT FROM THE CONFIG CARD
00004         *       INIT  LOAD MAP, PLACE GHOSTS, COUNT PELLETS
00005         *       MOVE  ONE TURN (REQ-DIR, REQ-CHAR)   QUIT  EOF
00006         * RULES : SEE DOCS/DECISIONS.MD D7, D9, D11, D14.
00007          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00008          PROGRAM-ID. PACGAME.
00009          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00010          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00011          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00012          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00013          DATA DIVISION.
00014          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00015          01  MAP-REQ COPY PACMREQ.
00016 C       * MAP SERVICE REQUEST: M-OP = LOAD WALL CLR  EAT  CNT
00017 C        01  MAP-REQ.
00018 C            05  M-OP                  PIC X(4).
00019 C            05  M-X                   PIC 99.
00020 C            05  M-Y                   PIC 99.
00021 C            05  M-FLAG                PIC 9.
00022 C            05  M-COUNT               PIC 999.
00023          01  GS-DATA               PIC X(15) VALUE '190930209419023'.
00024          01  GS-TABLE REDEFINES GS-DATA.
00025              05  GS-ENTRY          OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00026                  10  GS-X          PIC 99.
00027                  10  GS-Y          PIC 99.
00028                  10  GS-DIR        PIC 9.
00029          01  WS-G                  PIC 9.
00030          01  NEW-X                 PIC 99.
00031          01  NEW-Y                 PIC 99.
00032          LINKAGE SECTION.
00033          01  PAC-STATE COPY PACSTATE.
00034 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00035 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00036 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00037 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00038 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00039 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.
00040 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00041 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00042 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00043 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00044 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00045 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00046 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00047 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00048 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00049 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00050 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00051 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00052 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00053 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00054 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.   2


00055 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00056 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00057 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00058 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00059 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00060 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00061          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00062 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00063 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00064 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00065 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00066 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00067 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00068 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00069 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00070 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00071 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00072          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00073          MAIN-PARA.
00074              IF REQ-OP = 'RSET'
00075                  PERFORM DO-RESET
00076              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CFG '
00077                  PERFORM DO-CONFIG
00078              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00079                  PERFORM DO-INIT
00080              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'MOVE'
00081                  PERFORM DO-MOVE
00082              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'QUIT'
00083                  PERFORM DO-QUIT
00084              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'EOF '
00085                  MOVE 1 TO F-EOF.
00086              GOBACK.
00087          DO-RESET.
00088              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X.
00089              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y.
00090              MOVE 0 TO TICK-COUNT.
00091              MOVE 0 TO SCORE.
00092              MOVE 0 TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00093              MOVE 3 TO LIVES.
00094              MOVE 'READY  ' TO GAME-STATE.
00095              MOVE ' ' TO LAST-CMD.
00096              MOVE 'START  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00097              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00098              MOVE 0 TO F-LOST.
00099              MOVE 0 TO F-QUIT.
00100              MOVE 0 TO F-EOF.
00101              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00102              MOVE 1 TO AI-LEVEL.
00103              MOVE 1 TO GHOST-COUNT.
00104              MOVE 12345 TO GHOST-SEED.
00105          DO-CONFIG.
00106              MOVE REQ-LEVEL TO AI-LEVEL.
00107              MOVE REQ-COUNT TO GHOST-COUNT.
00108          DO-INIT.
00109              MOVE 'LOAD' TO M-OP.
00110              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00111              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.   3


00112              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00113              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00115              PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00116                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00117              PERFORM CLEAR-GHOST-CELL VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00118                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00119              MOVE 'CNT ' TO M-OP.
00120              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00121              MOVE M-COUNT TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00122          RESET-GHOST.
00123              MOVE GS-X (WS-G) TO GH-X (WS-G).
00124              MOVE GS-Y (WS-G) TO GH-Y (WS-G).
00125              MOVE GS-DIR (WS-G) TO GH-DIR (WS-G).
00126          CLEAR-GHOST-CELL.
00127              MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO M-X.
00128              MOVE GH-Y (WS-G) TO M-Y.
00129              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00130              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00131          DO-QUIT.
00132              MOVE 1 TO F-QUIT.
00133              MOVE 'QUIT   ' TO GAME-STATE.
00134          DO-MOVE.
00135              MOVE REQ-CHAR TO LAST-CMD.
00136              MOVE PLAYER-X TO NEW-X.
00137              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO NEW-Y.
00138              MOVE 'MOVED  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00139              PERFORM AIM-MOVE THRU AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00140              ADD 1 TO TICK-COUNT.
00141              MOVE NEW-X TO M-X.
00142              MOVE NEW-Y TO M-Y.
00143              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00144              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00145              IF M-FLAG = 1
00146                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO LAST-RESULT
00147              ELSE
00148                  MOVE NEW-X TO PLAYER-X
00149                  MOVE NEW-Y TO PLAYER-Y
00150                  PERFORM EAT-PELLET THRU EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00151              PERFORM ROUND-END THRU ROUND-END-EXIT.
00152              PERFORM SET-STATE THRU SET-STATE-EXIT.
00153          AIM-MOVE.
00154              IF REQ-DIR = 'U'
00155                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-Y
00156              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'D'
00157                  ADD 1 TO NEW-Y
00158              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'L'
00159                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-X
00160              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'R'
00161                  ADD 1 TO NEW-X.
00162          AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00163              EXIT.
00164          EAT-PELLET.
00165              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00166              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00167              MOVE 'EAT ' TO M-OP.
00168              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.   4


00169              IF M-FLAG = 1
00170                  ADD 10 TO SCORE
00171                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM PELLETS-LEFT.
00172              IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00173                  MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00174          EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00175              EXIT.
00176          ROUND-END.
00177              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00178              PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00179              IF F-HIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 0
00180                  CALL 'PACAI' USING PAC-STATE
00181                  PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00182              IF F-HIT = 1
00183                  PERFORM LOSE-LIFE THRU LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00184          ROUND-END-EXIT.
00185              EXIT.
00186          CHECK-HIT.
00187              PERFORM HIT-TEST THRU HIT-TEST-EXIT
00188                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00189          CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00190              EXIT.
00191          HIT-TEST.
00192              IF GH-X (WS-G) = PLAYER-X AND GH-Y (WS-G) = PLAYER-Y
00193                  MOVE 1 TO F-HIT.
00194          HIT-TEST-EXIT.
00195              EXIT.
00196          LOSE-LIFE.
00197              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00198              SUBTRACT 1 FROM LIVES.
00199              IF LIVES = 0
00200                  MOVE 1 TO F-LOST
00201                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00202              ELSE
00203                  MOVE 'DIED   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00204                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X
00205                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y
00206                  PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00207                      UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT
00208                  IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00209                      MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00210          LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00211              EXIT.
00212          SET-STATE.
00213              IF F-LOST = 1
00214                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO GAME-STATE
00215              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00216                  MOVE 'WON    ' TO GAME-STATE
00217              ELSE
00218                  MOVE 'RUNNING' TO GAME-STATE.
00219          SET-STATE-EXIT.
00220              EXIT.   5

00065          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00066          MAIN-PARA.
00067              MOVE 0 TO M-FLAG.
00068              IF M-OP = 'LOAD'
00069                  MOVE MAP-DATA TO MAP-AREA
00070              ELSE IF M-OP = 'WALL'
00071                  PERFORM DO-WALL THRU DO-WALL-EXIT
00072              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CLR '
00073                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00074              ELSE IF M-OP = 'EAT '
00075                  PERFORM DO-EAT THRU DO-EAT-EXIT
00076              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CNT '
00077                  PERFORM DO-COUNT.
00078              GOBACK.
00079          DO-WALL.
00080              IF M-X < 1 OR M-X > 20 OR M-Y < 1 OR M-Y > 10
00081                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG
00082              ELSE IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '#'
00083                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00084          DO-WALL-EXIT.
00085              EXIT.
00086          DO-EAT.
00087              IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '.'
00088                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00089                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00090          DO-EAT-EXIT.
00091              EXIT.
00092          DO-COUNT.
00093              MOVE 0 TO M-COUNT.
00094              PERFORM COUNT-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00095                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00096          COUNT-ROW.
00097              PERFORM COUNT-CELL THRU COUNT-CELL-EXIT
00098                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00099          COUNT-CELL.
00100              IF MAP-CELL (WS-ROW WS-COL) = '.'
00101                  ADD 1 TO M-COUNT.
00102          COUNT-CELL-EXIT.
00103              EXIT.   3

00001         * PACAI : GHOST MOVEMENT FOR ALL ACTIVE GHOSTS (PORTABLE).
00002         * AI-LEVEL 1 RANDOM, 2 PERSISTENT DIRECTION, 3 CHASE (MANHATTAN).
00003         * LCG SEED IN THE STATE (FIXED 12345) KEEPS RUNS REPRODUCIBLE.
00004         * WALL TESTS GO THROUGH PACMAP. NO NESTED SUBSCRIPTS (D13).
00005          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00006          PROGRAM-ID. PACAI.

00034          01  DIR-TABLE REDEFINES DIR-DATA.

00044          01  DIST-Y                PIC S99.

00049 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00050 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00051 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00052 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00053 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00054 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.   2


00055 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00056 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.

00059 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00060 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00061 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00062 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00063 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00064 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00065 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00066 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00067 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00068 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00069 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00070 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00071 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00072 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00073 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00074 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00075 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00076          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE.
00077          MAIN-PARA.
00078              PERFORM GHOST-MOVE THRU GHOST-MOVE-EXIT
00079                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00080              GOBACK.
00081          GHOST-MOVE.
00082              MOVE 0 TO CAND-N.
00083              MOVE GH-DIR (WS-G) TO WS-CUR.
00084              MOVE REV-D (WS-CUR) TO WS-REVD.
00085              PERFORM GHOST-PROBE THRU GHOST-PROBE-EXIT
00086                  VARYING WS-D FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-D > 4.
00087              IF CAND-N = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 1
00088                  PERFORM TRY-REVERSE THRU TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00089              IF CAND-N > 0
00090                  PERFORM CHOOSE-DIR THRU CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT
00091                  PERFORM STEP-GHOST.
00092          GHOST-MOVE-EXIT.
00093              EXIT.
00094          GHOST-PROBE.
00095              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00096              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00097              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.
00098              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00099              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00100              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00101              IF M-FLAG = 0
00102                  IF AI-LEVEL = 1 OR WS-D NOT = WS-REVD
00103                      ADD 1 TO CAND-N
00104                      MOVE WS-D TO CAND (CAND-N).
00105          GHOST-PROBE-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.
00107          TRY-REVERSE.
00108              MOVE WS-REVD TO WS-D.
00109              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00110              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00111              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.   3


00112              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00113              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00115              IF M-FLAG = 0
00116                  MOVE 1 TO CAND-N
00117                  MOVE WS-D TO CAND (1).
00118          TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00119              EXIT.
00120          CHOOSE-DIR.
00121              IF AI-LEVEL = 3
00122                  PERFORM PICK-CHASE
00123              ELSE IF AI-LEVEL = 2 AND CAND-N = 1
00124                  MOVE CAND (1) TO WS-D
00125              ELSE
00126                  PERFORM PICK-RANDOM.
00127          CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT.
00128              EXIT.
00129          PICK-RANDOM.
00130              COMPUTE WS-WORK = GHOST-SEED * 75 + 74.
00131              DIVIDE WS-WORK BY 65537 GIVING WS-QUOT
00132                  REMAINDER GHOST-SEED.
00133              DIVIDE GHOST-SEED BY CAND-N GIVING WS-QUOT
00134                  REMAINDER WS-REM.
00135              ADD 1 TO WS-REM.
00136              MOVE CAND (WS-REM) TO WS-D.
00137          PICK-CHASE.
00138              MOVE 999 TO BEST-DIST.
00139              PERFORM CHASE-EVAL THRU CHASE-EVAL-EXIT
00140                  VARYING WS-J FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-J > CAND-N.
00141          CHASE-EVAL.
00142              MOVE CAND (WS-J) TO WS-K.
00143              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-K) - 1.
00144              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-K) - 1.
00145              COMPUTE DIST-X = G-NX - PLAYER-X.
00146              IF DIST-X < 0
00147                  COMPUTE DIST-X = 0 - DIST-X.
00148              COMPUTE DIST-Y = G-NY - PLAYER-Y.
00149              IF DIST-Y < 0
00150                  COMPUTE DIST-Y = 0 - DIST-Y.
00151              COMPUTE DIST-T = DIST-X + DIST-Y.
00152              IF DIST-T < BEST-DIST
00153                  MOVE DIST-T TO BEST-DIST
00154                  MOVE WS-K TO WS-D.
00155          CHASE-EVAL-EXIT.
00156              EXIT.
00157          STEP-GHOST.
00158              COMPUTE GH-X (WS-G) = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00159              COMPUTE GH-Y (WS-G) = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00160              MOVE WS-D TO GH-DIR (WS-G).   4



*STATISTICS*     SOURCE RECORDS =   160     DATA DIVISION STATEMENTS =    59     PROCEDURE DIVISION STATEMENTS =    66

00001         * PACINPT : INPUT ADAPTER. ONE SYSIN CARD -> ONE REQUEST.
00002         * OPS : OPEN  READ  CLOS  PARM (CLASSIFY REQ-TEXT, NO I/O).
00003         * REQ-KIND: MOV (REQ-DIR U/D/L/R)  QUI  CFG (REQ-LEVEL,
00004         *   REQ-COUNT)  INV  EOF, AND IN TSO MODE RDR (BLANK LINE =
00005         *   REDRAW REQUEST). REQ-CHAR HOLDS THE COMMAND'S FIRST BYTE.
00006         * ONLY THIS MODULE KNOWS ABOUT THE TERMINAL/CARD SOURCE.
00007          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00008          PROGRAM-ID. PACINPT.
00009          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00010          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00011          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00012          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00013          INPUT-OUTPUT SECTION.
00014          FILE-CONTROL.
00015              SELECT COMMAND-FILE ASSIGN TO UT-S-SYSIN.
00016          DATA DIVISION.
00017          FILE SECTION.
00018          FD  COMMAND-FILE
00019              RECORDING MODE IS F
00020              LABEL RECORDS ARE OMITTED
00021              RECORD CONTAINS 80 CHARACTERS
00022              DATA RECORD IS COMMAND-RECORD.
00023          01  COMMAND-RECORD        PIC X(80).
00024          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00025          01  WS-CARD.
00026              05  CMD-CHAR          PIC X.
00027              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00028              05  CMD-LEVEL         PIC X.
00029              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00030              05  CMD-COUNT         PIC X.
00031              05  FILLER            PIC X(75).
00032          01  RAW-CARD REDEFINES WS-CARD.
00033              05  RAW-CHAR          PIC X OCCURS 80 TIMES.
00034          01  NORM-REC.
00035              05  NR-CHAR           PIC X OCCURS 80 TIMES.
00036          01  WS-OFF                PIC 99.
00037          01  WS-I                  PIC 99.
00038          01  WS-J                  PIC 99.
00039          01  WS-LIM                PIC 99.
00040          LINKAGE SECTION.
00041          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00042 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00043 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00044 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00045 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00046 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00047 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00048 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00049 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00050 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00051 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00052          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ.
00053          MAIN-PARA.
00054              IF REQ-OP = 'OPEN'   2


00055                  OPEN INPUT COMMAND-FILE
00056              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ'
00057                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00058              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLOS'
00059                  CLOSE COMMAND-FILE
00060              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PARM'
00061                  MOVE SPACES TO WS-CARD
00062                  MOVE REQ-TEXT TO WS-CARD
00063                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00064              GOBACK.
00065          DO-READ.
00066              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-KIND.
00067              READ COMMAND-FILE AT END MOVE 'EOF' TO REQ-KIND.
00068              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00069                  MOVE COMMAND-RECORD TO WS-CARD.
00070              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00071                  PERFORM NORMALIZE THRU NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00072              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00073                                   AND WS-CARD = SPACES
00074                  MOVE 'RDR' TO REQ-KIND.
00075              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00076                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00077          DO-READ-EXIT.
00078              EXIT.
00079          NORMALIZE.
00080         * TSO ONLY: TOLERATE LEADING BLANKS/NON-PRINTABLE BYTES TYPED
00081         * BEFORE THE COMMAND: START AT THE FIRST BYTE > BLANK (1-8).
00082              MOVE 1 TO WS-OFF.
00083              PERFORM FIND-START UNTIL WS-OFF > 8
00084                  OR RAW-CHAR (WS-OFF) > SPACE.
00085              IF WS-OFF > 1 AND WS-OFF < 9
00086                  PERFORM SHIFT-LEFT THRU SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00087          NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00088              EXIT.
00089          FIND-START.
00090              ADD 1 TO WS-OFF.
00091          SHIFT-LEFT.
00092              MOVE SPACES TO NORM-REC.
00093              COMPUTE WS-LIM = 81 - WS-OFF.
00094              PERFORM COPY-CHAR VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1
00095                  UNTIL WS-I > WS-LIM.
00096              MOVE NORM-REC TO WS-CARD.
00097          SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00098              EXIT.
00099          COPY-CHAR.
00100              COMPUTE WS-J = WS-I + WS-OFF - 1.
00101              MOVE RAW-CHAR (WS-J) TO NR-CHAR (WS-I).
00102          CLASSIFY.
00103              MOVE CMD-CHAR TO REQ-CHAR.
00104              MOVE 'INV' TO REQ-KIND.
00105              IF CMD-CHAR = 'U' OR CMD-CHAR = 'u'
00106                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00107                  MOVE 'U' TO REQ-DIR
00108              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'D' OR CMD-CHAR = 'd'
00109                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00110                  MOVE 'D' TO REQ-DIR
00111              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'L' OR CMD-CHAR = 'l'   3


00112                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00113                  MOVE 'L' TO REQ-DIR
00114              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'R' OR CMD-CHAR = 'r'
00115                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00116                  MOVE 'R' TO REQ-DIR
00117              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'Q' OR CMD-CHAR = 'q'
00118                  MOVE 'QUI' TO REQ-KIND
00119              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'A'
00120                  MOVE 'CFG' TO REQ-KIND
00121                  PERFORM SET-CONFIG THRU SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00122          CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00123              EXIT.
00124          SET-CONFIG.
00125              IF CMD-LEVEL = '0'
00126                  MOVE 0 TO REQ-LEVEL
00127              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '2'
00128                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-LEVEL
00129              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '3'
00130                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-LEVEL
00131              ELSE
00132                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-LEVEL.
00133              IF CMD-COUNT = '2'
00134                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-COUNT
00135              ELSE IF CMD-COUNT = '3'
00136                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-COUNT
00137              ELSE
00138                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-COUNT.
00139          SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00140              EXIT.   4

00001         * PACPLAT : PLATFORM ADAPTER (MVS 3.8J). OPS : INIT FINI CLS.
00002         * INIT validates the requested REQ-MODE (BAT cards or TSO
00003         * interactive); anything else falls back to BAT. FINI sets the
00004         * return code. CLS (TSO only) makes the next line start a
00005         * fresh screen via PACTSO, avoiding '***' paging; in batch
00006         * PACTSO does nothing. GAME/MAP/AI never see any of this.
